# ci(lint): run pre-commit only on changed files for pull requests

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/47847) · state: closed · merged: False

## Original PR description

## Summary

Speed up the `Lint` CI workflow by running pre-commit only over the files changed by a pull request, instead of the whole repository.

## Motivation

`.github/workflows/lint.yml` currently runs:

```yaml
pre-commit run --all-files --show-diff-on-failure
```

`--all-files` scans every matching file in the repo even though a PR usually touches only a few. As the repo grows this step keeps getting slower, and pre-existing formatting issues in unrelated files can fail a PR's lint and add noise for contributors.

## Changes

The `Run pre-commit checks` step now branches on the event type:

- **`pull_request`** — lint only the PR's changed files via `--from-ref <base.sha> --to-ref <head.sha>`.
- **`push`** (e.g. `main`) — no diff range is available, so it falls back to `--all-files`.

This relies on the existing checkout `fetch-depth: 0`: PRs check out the merge commit, so both `base.sha` and `head.sha` are reachable locally. pre-commit natively supports `--from-ref/--to-ref` and runs hooks only on files changed between the refs that match each hook's `files` rule.

## Scope / Non-goals

- Only the pre-commit step of the `Lint` workflow is changed. The lychee, cookbook, and mint broken-links checks are untouched.
- Trunk quality is preserved: `push` to `main` still runs the full-repo check.

## Testing

- [ ] Open a PR that changes a few Python files → lint reports only those files.
- [ ] Open a PR that changes only docs / non-Python files → not blocked by unrelated Python formatting issues.
- [ ] Push to `main` → pre-commit still runs across the whole repo.
